\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime},\Gamma^{\prime\prime}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]\vdash(r[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}])(\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i.):A[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]
. Finally,
A[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]\equiv A[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]
by
Lemma

A.7
.

∎

Proposition A.4
.

The unit operation is admissible on context telescopes, types and terms.

\frac{\Gamma,i:\mathbb{T},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}\vdash\Gamma^{\prime}\,\,\mathsf{tele}}{\Gamma\vdash\Gamma^{\prime}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]\,\,\mathsf{tele}}\quad\textsc{left=unit-tele,fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}\frac{\Gamma,i:\mathbb{T},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash a:A}{\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]\vdash Now, the proofs of the equations used in the previous two
propositions, all of which hold on the level of well-scoped raw terms.
For each equation, the variable rule is the only case where something
interesting happens.

Lemma A.5
.

\frac{G_{1}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash s\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{J}\in\mathsf{locks}(G_{2})\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},G^{\prime}\vdash t\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{L}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime})\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash s^{\prime\prime}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{K}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime})}{G_{1},G_{2},G^{\prime},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\vec{s}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{t}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

Proof.

Suppose
G_{1}\equiv G_{11},x,G_{12}
and the variable usage is

\frac{G_{11},x,G_{12}\vdash m\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{M}\in\mathsf{locks}(G_{12})}{G_{11},x,G_{12}\vdash x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}}\rrbracket\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}

Then

\displaystyle x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}}\rrbracket[\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}[\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}[\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{s}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{t}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\begin{aligned} &\vec{m}[\vec{s}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{t}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\\
&\vec{s}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\\
&\vec{t}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\\
&\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}}\rrbracket[\vec{s}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{t}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

by inductive hypothesis on
m
.
∎

Lemma A.6
.

\frac{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\quad\quad G,G^{\prime}\vdash t\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{L}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime})\quad\quad G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash s^{\prime\prime}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{K}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime}_{2})}{G,G^{\prime},G^{\prime\prime}_{1},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

Proof.

There are two places where the variable may lie.

Case:

If
x
lies in
G
, so
G\equiv G_{1},x,G_{2}
:

\frac{G_{1},x,G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}_{1}\vdash m\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{M}\in\mathsf{locks}(G_{2})\quad\quad G_{1},x,G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}_{1}\vdash n\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{N}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime}_{1})}{G_{1},x,G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}_{1}\vdash x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}

And then

\displaystyle x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\begin{aligned} &\vec{m}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\\
&\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\\
&\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\\
&\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket\end{aligned}

Each of these keys attached to
x
can be rewritten by induction:

\displaystyle\vec{m}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv\vec{m}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

And so, continuing the chain of equations:

\displaystyle x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv\dots

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\begin{aligned} &\vec{m}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\\
&\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\\
&\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\\
&\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

Case:

If
x
lies in
G_{1}^{\prime\prime}
, so
G_{1}^{\prime\prime}\equiv G_{11}^{\prime\prime},x,G_{12}^{\prime\prime}
:

\frac{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{11},x,G^{\prime\prime}_{12}\vdash n\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{N}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime}_{12})}{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{11},x,G^{\prime\prime}_{12}\vdash x\llbracket\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}

The reasoning is essentially the same:

\displaystyle x\llbracket\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\begin{aligned} &\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\\
&\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}},\\
&\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}\rrbracket\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{t}[\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{n}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv x\llbracket\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime\prime}}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

again by induction in the middle step.

∎

Lemma A.7
.

The unit and counit commute, depending on the relative position in
the context the two operations are being applied to.

\frac{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\quad\quad G,G^{\prime}\vdash t\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{L}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime})}{G,G^{\prime},G^{\prime\prime}_{1},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

\frac{G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\quad\quad G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime}\vdash s^{\prime}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{K}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime})}{G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime}}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

Proof.

For the first, there are several places a variable could be, but the case of
interest is the variable rule for
i
:

\frac{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash k\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{K}\quad\quad G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash o\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{O}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime}_{2})}{G,G^{\prime\prime}_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}},G^{\prime\prime}_{2}\vdash i\llbracket k/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{o}/{\mathcal{O}}\rrbracket\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}

in which case

\displaystyle i\llbracket k/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{o}/{\mathcal{O}}\rrbracket[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv i\llbracket\vec{t}[k[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{o}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{O}}]/{\mathcal{L}},k[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{o}[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{O}}\rrbracket[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv k[\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv k[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv i\llbracket k/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{o}/{\mathcal{O}}\rrbracket[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}][\vec{t}/{\mathcal{L}}]

For the second, again the variable rule for
i
is the interesting case:

\frac{G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash l\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{L}\quad\quad G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash m\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{M}\in\mathsf{locks}(G_{2})\quad\quad G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash n\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{N}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime\prime})}{G_{1},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G_{2},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash i\llbracket l/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

in which case

\displaystyle i\llbracket l/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv i\llbracket l[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{m}[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{s^{\prime}}[\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}}]/{\mathcal{K}},\vec{n}[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}]/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv l[\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]

\displaystyle\equiv l[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime}}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

\displaystyle\equiv i\llbracket l/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{m}/{\mathcal{M}},\vec{n}/{\mathcal{N}}\rrbracket[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}][\vec{s^{\prime}}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]

∎

Lemma A.8
.

\frac{G_{1}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G^{\prime}\vdash s\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{M}\in\mathsf{locks}(G_{2})\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G^{\prime}\vdash l\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{L}\quad\quad G_{1},G_{2},i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G^{\prime}\vdash s^{\prime}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}\text{ for }\mathcal{N}\in\mathsf{locks}(G^{\prime})}{G_{1},G_{2},G^{\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\vec{s}/{\mathcal{J}},l/{\mathcal{L}},\vec{s^{\prime}}/{\mathcal{K}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\vec{s}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{J}},\vec{s^{\prime}}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]/{\mathcal{K}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

Proof.

Straightforward induction, key is that
i
does not occur in
\mathcal{J}
.
∎

Lemma A.9
.

\frac{G,i,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}},G^{\prime},k,\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}\,\,\mathsf{rawterm}}{G,G^{\prime},G^{\prime\prime}\vdash\mathcal{J}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}k./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}]\equiv\mathcal{J}[\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}k./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{K}}][\raisebox{-0.5pt}{\lx@scalerel@obj{\reflectbox{\rotatebox[origin={c}]{185.0}{$\lambda$}}}}{}i./\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.8}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.5}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}{\scalebox{0.4}{\text{\faIconFromMacro{faLock}}}}_{\mathcal{L}}]}\quad\textsc{fraction={-{\,-\,}-}}

Proof.

The interesting cases are, of course, the variable rules for
i
and
k
